# P4: Cole reference register (Phase 4a)

Every reference to the Cole household or to a v4 case fact outside the injected CASE block, in the files Phase 4b migrates. Built on branch next-term-2027 from eight hit-driven scans (check 1c of verify-migration, Part K grep targets, COLE keys, v4 topic words, data arrays, alt/aria/title/data attributes), one per file group, then read as a whole. Excluded as the runbook directs: session-0.1 (Phase 8 rebuilds it) and the record files (CHANGELOG.md, changelog/, audit/, docs/changes/, docs/audits/). Dispositions follow the runbook: KEEP-NAME, REWRITE-V5, REPLACE-EXERCISE, DELETE, RECAPTURE. The R2 calls behind each REPLACE-EXERCISE and RECAPTURE are logged in DECISIONS.md.

## Units behind the counts

Rows count lines, not teaching units. Read the two large counts this way:

| Disposition | Rows | Units | What the units are |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| RECAPTURE | 137 | 8 runs | session-2 `t-s8` (the questions the model asked back about the memo); session-3 `offP0` (follow-up letter) and `offP1` (task list) on the v5 NTSUM payload; docs/probe-captures.md `P1` run three ways (`P1` search off, `P1b` search on, `P4` bare API), `P2` (follow-up email) and `P3` (the schedule comparison, 89 rows) |
| REPLACE-EXERCISE | 50 | 6 exercises | session-1 `s12` tier-quiz items 5 and 6; session-2 `s8` builder-assembler (the memo interview) and `s10` citation triage with its `CITES` array (26 of the rows); session-3 `s4` the four retriever presets; instructor-notes/session-2.md mirrors of the session-2 changes |

R6 handling of the eight runs (D-033): Phase 4b re-runs `t-s8`, `offP0` and `offP1` through `scripts/recapture.mjs` and labels each with the model id the CLI reported and the date. The five docs/probe-captures.md runs serve Session 0.1's probe exercise only, which Phase 8 rebuilds; they are deferred to Phase 8 with the page, and Phase 4b's hub run rewrites the file's banner so it says which case version the captures were made against and that a re-run is pending.

R2 handling of the six exercises (D-032): all six are accepted as proposed, same component family, same section id, same position and minutes. Every authority a replacement item names must already be in CASE.md Part I or SOURCES.md, or the item is written without it; no authority is added from memory (R7).

Scope notes from the scans: session-0.1 and the record files were excluded as instructed; instructor-notes/session-0.1.md and session-1.md were scanned (one row in session-1.md, none in session-0.1.md beyond what Phase 8 owns); docs/probe-captures.md is outside `verify-migration` check 1c's served surface, so its v4 strings do not fail the gate, and its banner carries the version honesty instead.
